One startup account per person effectively; existing enterprise accounts can't redeem.
  - *Source:* https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/free/students
- **Microsoft for Startups Investor Network track (~$100,000+ Azure credits, referral-gated)** (high) — From the MFS FAQ read today: investor-backed startups enter with 'most startups begin with $100,000 [in Azure credits], with the opportunity to earn more' plus Azure Standard Support, technical experts, go-to-market support, and free/discounted third-party benefits: GitHub Enterprise, LinkedIn Premium Business, Microsoft 365 Business Premium, Visual Studio Enterprise, Dynamics 365, and Power Platform. Referral codes come from affiliated investors, accelerators, incubators, angel networks, or universities in the Investor Network. This track started at $100,000 when the 2025 two-track system launched; amounts can vary by referral source and engagement.
  - *Eligibility:* Startup must be affiliated with a Microsoft for Startups Investor Network partner AND hold that partner's referral code. Company must otherwise meet MFS eligibility (for-profit, privately held, not Series C or later, < $350k lifetime free Azure credits). Universities now also count as referral sources, so a university-backed startup can use this path.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Get a referral code from your affiliated investor/accelator/incubator/university. 2) Enter the code during the startups.microsoft.com application. 3) Upon approval, activate credits and redeem third-party benefits in the MFS portal (Azure Portal > Get started/benefits).
  - *Expiry:* Same rules as Startup credits: activate within 90 days of agreement; valid up to 2 years once activated. Third-party benefits (GitHub Enterprise etc.) have their own redemption windows.
  - *Gotchas:* Without a referral code you cap out at the self-serve/milestone path. Benefits can't be redeemed on accounts that already redeemed the same benefit or on existing enterprise accounts. Pegasus (dedicated architect/sales program) continued after the 2025 changes but is invitation-only and no longer referenced in current FAQ — do not plan around it.
  - *Source:* https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/startups/microsoft-for-startups/mfs-faqs
- **Azure Free Account (open to everyone, incl. students) — $200 trial + 12-month free services** (high) — Read today at azure.microsoft.com/free: $200 credit usable within 30 days; free monthly amounts of 20+ popular services for 12 months (e.g., 750 hrs each of B1s/B2pts v2/B2ats v2 VMs, 32 GB PostgreSQL/MySQL storage, 720 vCore-hrs SQL Managed Instance, 100 GB Container Registry, 5 GB Cloud Shell/Storage); plus 65+ always-free services (e.g., 0.5M Speech chars/mo, 500 pages/mo Document Intelligence, 50 MB AI Search, 5,000 Vision transactions/mo). One free account per person. Spending protection: card won't be billed while covered; $1 temporary authorization possible.
  - *Eligibility:* New Azure customers, worldwide (card policy varies: HK and Brazil accept only credit cards). Students can claim this too, but if you're a student the Azure for Students offer below is better (no card, annual renewal). Not for people who already have a paid Azure account.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Sign up at azure.microsoft.com/free with Microsoft account. 2) Add a phone number and a credit/debit card (non-prepaid). 3) $200 credit applies immediately; free monthly amounts auto-attach. 4) After 30 days you're notified to convert to pay-as-you-go or the account is disabled.
  - *Expiry:* $200 credit: 30 days (unused credit does not extend, even if you convert early). Free monthly service amounts: 12 months, then pay-as-you-go rates apply. Always-free tiers have no time limit but usage is capped.
  - *Gotchas:* Trial, not free: card required, and pay-as-you-go starts the moment the credit or the 12-month window ends. Free credit cannot be applied to Azure Marketplace offers, Spot VMs, or Hybrid Benefit. 6-month AI-services window previously advertised is no longer on the page — only the 12-month/always-free tiers are.
  - *Source:* https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/free
- **Azure for Students — $100 credit for 12 months, no credit card (school email required)** (high) — Read today at azure.microsoft.com/free/students: $100 in Azure credit usable within 12 months, NO credit card required; free monthly amounts of 20+ popular services for 12 months plus 65+ always-free services; full service catalog up to free amounts; 'renew your subscription annually and continue to get free access to Azure as long as you're a student'. A 'Starter' tier for younger users gives free service access only (no credit). The GitHub Student Developer Pack also routes 18+ students to this $100 offer (ages 13–17 get a reduced service set instead: App Service, Functions, Notification Hubs, MySQL for apps, App Insights, DevOps).
  - *Eligibility:* Full-time university students; sign up with a school (.edu/institutional) email for verification. No card needed. One account per person. Age: the pack routes 13–17 to Starter; the $100 credit is for 18+. Third-party trackers (checked Oct 2026) list country restrictions — some regions only allow Starter; check the signup flow.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Sign up at azure.microsoft.com/free/students using your school email (or via the 'Microsoft Azure' offer in the GitHub Student Developer Pack after verification). 2) Confirm student status. 3) $100 credit activates immediately; free service amounts attach. 4) Renew annually while enrolled.
  - *Expiry:* $100 credit valid 12 months from signup; annual renewal keeps the free services going while you remain a full-time student. 12-month free-service amounts reset with the renewal.
  - *Gotchas:* The $100 credit is the same offer the GitHub Pack grants — don't claim it twice (one Azure account per person; a second claim fails or voids the benefit). No card = no pay-as-you-go fallback: when the credit or free amounts run out, resources stop rather than bill you. Students who need more than free amounts should use the MFS self-serve $1,000 offer instead (which does need a card).
  - *Source:* https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/free/students
- **GitHub Student Developer Pack — free Microsoft/educator stack (GitHub + M365 + Azure + tools)** (high) — Verified today on education.github.com/pack: while verified as a student you get free GitHub Pro, free GitHub Copilot 'Student' plan (unlimited completions + monthly GitHub AI credits, limited chat/agent on auto-selected models), 3-month Microsoft 365 Education (Word/Excel/PowerPoint/Teams/1TB OneDrive — duration on the current page says 'free or discounted', historically 3 months), $100 Azure credit (18+), Namecheap free .me domain (1 year) + free SSL, Heroku $13/month for 24 months, free annual-renewable JetBrains licenses, and a GitHub Foundations/Copilot certification voucher via Microsoft Learn (resumed Sept 2025). Enrollment requires being 13+, in a degree/diploma program; under-18s get a reduced offer set.
  - *Eligibility:* Enrolled students: verify via school (.edu) email OR student ID + one proof-of-enrollment document (or a .edu email alone in some regions); 13+; some individual offers (Azure $100, Heroku) require 18+. No country limit stated for the pack itself.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Apply at education.github.com/pack (or github.com/education) with school email or student ID. 2) Approval unlocks the pack page at github.com/features/benefits/students. 3) Redeem each partner offer individually (Azure, M365, Heroku, domain, etc.).
  - *Expiry:* Benefits last while you remain a verified student (GitHub Pro, M365, JetBrains renew annually/on graduation). Heroku credit: fixed 24 months. Domain/SSL: 1 year. Once you graduate, the pack stops renewing.
  - *Gotchas:* Partners churn: DigitalOcean ($200 credit) and Canva left the pack (DigitalOcean credits expire 2026-08-01), Bump.sh ended Dec 2025, Appwrite offer restructured to 6 months in Oct 2026. The Azure $100 in the pack IS the Azure for Students offer — one per person. 13–17-year-olds get no $100 credit, only a reduced service list.
  - *Source:* https://education.github.com/pack/
- **Microsoft 365 Education trial (30 days, schools) + free M365A license for institutions** (medium) — Microsoft offers schools a free 30-day Microsoft 365 Education trial (Word, Excel, PowerPoint, Teams, OneDrive, Class Notebook/whiteboard, Copilot in education apps, no credit card) claimable by an institution admin, and a free Microsoft 365 A1 (M365A) license for eligible K-12 schools and universities — through which most university students already get M365A at no personal cost via their school. Individual students at non-licensed institutions can use the free Office web apps/Word/Excel at no charge. (Official Microsoft page served localized/JS content and timed out, so the trial details are from the well-known program terms rather than a page read today — hence medium confidence.)
  - *Eligibility:* Trial: K-12 and higher-ed institutions (admin-managed). Free M365A: eligible institutions (K-12 schools and universities in supported countries); individual students get it only through an institutional license. No .edu email needed to sign the school up for the trial.
  - *How to claim:* 1) School: admin claims the 30-day trial from the M365 Education 'try it free' link on microsoft.com/en-us/education. 2) K-12: apply for the free M365A K-12 license via the Microsoft Education Store/partner flow. 3) Students: ask IT for the school's M365A license, or use free Office web apps with any email.

- **Azure AI / Microsoft Foundry always-free tiers (available to students and startups alike)** (high) — From the Azure Free and Azure for Students pages read today: monthly always-free AI service amounts included with any free/12-month account: Azure Speech in Foundry Tools 0.5M neural characters/month; Document Intelligence 500 pages/month (S0); AI Search 50 MB storage (10k docs, 3 indexes); Vision in Foundry Tools 5,000 transactions/month per S1-S3 tier; Content Safety 5,000 text records or images/month; Azure DevOps free for 5 users; PostgreSQL/MySQL serverless free tiers. These stack on top of credit programs — useful for AI workloads while credits burn out.

  - *How to claim:* No separate claim — free amounts auto-attach to eligible Azure services; monitor usage in Cost Management to stay under caps.
  - *Expiry:* Always-free: perpetual. 12-month amounts: until 12 months from account creation, then standard pricing.
  - *Gotchas:* Usage above the free amounts bills at normal rates (with no card, it just throttles/stops). Large AI inference (e.g., LLM tokens) is NOT in the always-free list — paid per token even in Foundry; budget accordingly.
  - *Source:* https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/free

Everything else above is confirmed from the official Microsoft pages read today (overview/application/FAQ dated 2026-05 to 2026-10; pricing + free-services + Azure for Students pages current as of 2026-10-22). ### Postman

- **Postman Free plan (free tier)** (high) — Official Free plan on postman.com/pricing (read 2026-10-22): 1 user; 1,000 API monitoring requests/month (per team); unlimited automated API testing via Collection Runner; Mock Servers (cloud and local) with no stated cap; 25 Automation Flows credits/month; Context Graph ingestion free (24h refresh) and 1,000 Context Graph queries/month then $0.01/query; up to 5 integrations; 10,000 calls/month to Postman's own platform API; collection recovery window 1 day; Postman-branded API docs. Not included on Free: RBAC, SSO, cloud performance testing, private NPM packages, SDK generation, custom domains, monitor reports, API Catalog, private runners. Card lists '100 AI credits' but the comparison table row 'Postman AI credits' shows '50 / month' for Free - a live discrepancy on the same page; known data (100 AI credits/mo) matches the plan card. Paid reference prices (annual billing): Solo $9/user/mo, Team $19/user/mo, Enterprise via sales.
  - *Eligibility:* Open to everyone - individuals, students, startups; no .edu email, age, funding, or region requirement stated. Free account creation (email or SSO login) with no payment method on the page; credit card is not part of the free sign-up (official page does not say 'no credit card required' - medium confidence on that detail). Free desktop app + CLI: 'Download Postman | Get Started for Free', 'Create a free account and you are in.'
  - *How to claim:* Go to postman.com/pricing -> 'Get Started'/'Sign Up for Free' to create a free account; the API client, CLI, and platform features at Free limits are immediately available. Download app at postman.com/downloads.
  - *Expiry:* Perpetual free tier; no trial clock or renewal. Free features stay until user adds a paid plan; downgrades keep the account usable at Free limits.
  - *Gotchas:* Only 1 user on Free (a free team workspace with collaborators requires a paid plan). The pricing page self-contradicts on AI credits (100 on plan card vs 50/month in the table) - verify before relying on 100. Usage-based charges can still hit a Free account: Context Graph overage $0.01/query beyond 1,000/month, and cloud perf testing / AI overage are paid features gated to paid plans. Basic and Professional plans are gone for new customers, so older student-startup offers built on those tiers no longer exist.
  - *Source:* https://www.postman.com/pricing/
- **Postman Academy (free education/certification platform)** (high) — academy.postman.com (read live 2026-10-22): 'Master APIs with Postman Academy' - free on-demand self-study learning modules and learning paths (e.g., API Testing Path v12, API Collaboration Best Practices), free live webinars ('Sign up for free, live sessions led by Postman trainers'), and course-completion badges/certifications. Stats shown: 150k+ learners, 250+ workshops, 95% satisfaction. Content can be filtered by persona and Postman license type. Enterprise training (private workshops, custom-branded academy, LMS reporting) is a paid/Enterprise offering, not part of the free offer.
  - *Eligibility:* Open to everyone - no .edu email, region, or startup requirement stated; access via a free Postman account. No credit card required.
  - *How to claim:* Sign in to Postman at academy.postman.com, pick a learning path or course, complete it; badges are issued automatically after course completion (now via Credly).
  - *Expiry:* Perpetual free access; no stated expiration. Badging migration: from 21 Jul 2026 badges move from Badgr to Credly; previously earned Badgr badges are NOT revoked; reissuance of old badges only on request to academy@postman.com.
  - *Gotchas:* Free tier is the self-study/live-webinar content; instructor-led private workshops and custom-branded academies are enterprise/commercial. Not a free Postman product license - it is free learning content. The 'new learning platform' LiftOff (postman.com/liftoff) is Postman's 2026 successor-style track alongside Academy.
  - *Source:* https://academy.postman.com/
- **Postman LiftOff (free hands-on learning, new in 2026)** (high) — postman.com/liftoff (read live 2026-10-22): 'Interactive, hands-on learning modules with real-time API validation', 'Powered by Postman'. Currently 5 Learning Paths and 21 Modules; progress steps per module (e.g., '0/20 steps completed'); users can 'earn your rank'. Sign in with Discord to save progress; browse without signing in. No pricing, paid tier, or limits stated anywhere on the page - free.
  - *Eligibility:* Open to everyone ('Start your API journey here'); no student/startup-specific targeting, no .edu or region requirement; Discord sign-in optional.
  - *How to claim:* Browse paths at postman.com/liftoff, sign in with Discord to track progress, connect Postman when ready to validate against real APIs.
  - *Expiry:* No expiration stated; free with a Postman account.
  - *Gotchas:* No certifications or credentials issued on the LiftOff page itself (unlike Academy badges); small catalog so far (5 paths / 21 modules) - content is being built out. 'Real-time API validation' consumes runs against your Postman account limits (Free plan applies).
  - *Source:* https://www.postman.com/liftoff
- **Postman for Students program (sunset/absent as of 2026-10-22)** (medium) — The former dedicated student offering has no live presence: https://www.postman.com/students/ returns HTTP 200 but is a generic app SPA shell (identical byte-size shell as /startups/ and /education/, title just 'Postman') - i.e., the marketing page no longer exists; /learn/education/ is a hard 404; the current Learn hub (postman.com/learn/, read 2026-10-22) contains no student/educator program links, only Docs, Academy, LiftOff, webinars, Discord. After the March 2026 plan restructure, the tiers are Free/Solo/Team/Enterprise with Basic and Professional 'no longer available for new customers', so the paid-tier student licenses that a student program would have granted no longer exist for new signups. Net effect: student access is covered by the universal Free plan (1 user) + free Academy/LiftOff learning. Note: web search budget was exhausted this session, so a sunset announcement page could not be located directly; conclusion is from live absence on official pages.
  - *Eligibility:* N/A - program not claimable; no .edu verification flow found anywhere on current official pages.

  - *Expiry:* No active program; old pages soft-404 to the app shell, /learn/education/ hard-404s.
  - *Gotchas:* Treat any third-party page or archive claiming a 'Postman for Students' free Professional license as outdated; Professional is not offered to new customers. If a school previously had an education license, it would be legacy Basic/Professional and is migrated to Team over time per Postman FAQ.
  - *Source:* https://www.postman.com/students/(soft-404toappshell)
- **Startup program / startup discount (not found)** (medium) — No public 'Postman for Startups' program exists on official pages as of 2026-10-22: https://www.postman.com/startups/ returns 200 but serves the same generic app SPA shell as other nonexistent marketing routes; the pricing page and FAQ contain no startup discount, funding-cap, or company-age terms; the only 'trial' wording is 'Teams can trial Enterprise features to evaluate advanced collaboration, security, and governance before upgrading' (Enterprise evaluation via sales, not a public self-serve startup offer). Postman's partner-facing program (/partner-program/) is for technology/integration partners, not for startups. Absence verified by live page fetches; no web search was possible this session, so a non-public/VC-channel startup deal cannot be fully ruled out.
  - *Eligibility:* N/A - no public startup offer to claim.
  - *How to claim:* N/A - startups, like all users, get the standard Free plan (1 user) and would pay Solo $9/user/mo or Team $19/user/mo (annual) for more; Enterprise evaluation by contacting sales.

- **Trial credits** (medium) — No self-serve trial-credit program for students or startups. The pricing FAQ (read 2026-10-22) only states: 'Teams can trial Enterprise features to evaluate advanced collaboration, security, and governance before upgrading' - no duration or self-serve steps published. The Free plan itself is not a trial: it is a perpetual tier (1 user, 1,000 monitoring requests/month, unlimited collection runs, mock servers), with no credit card required. AI overage is pay-as-you-go on paid plans only ($0.035-$0.05/credit depending on tier).
  - *Eligibility:* Free plan: everyone, no CC. Enterprise trial: organizations via sales contact; no student/startup-specific trial credits.
  - *How to claim:* Free plan: sign up at postman.com with a free account. Enterprise trial: 'Contact Sales' from postman.com/pricing.
  - *Expiry:* Free tier: no expiry. Enterprise trial: duration not published.
  - *Gotchas:* Don't confuse Free tier with an expiring trial - it is a permanent plan. Usage-based add-ons (Context Graph overage $0.01/query, cloud perf testing $0.04/VuH on Solo) can generate charges on paid plans even though the base is free.
  - *Source:* https://www.postman.com/pricing/(FAQ)
- **2025–26 change:** 2025-2026 changes (verified on official pages 2026-10-22): (1) March 2026 plan consolidation - Postman rebuilt tiers to Free/Solo/Team/Enterprise with an AI-native feature set shipping March 1, 2026 (v11/v12); per pricing FAQ, 'Basic and Professional plans are no longer available for new customers', existing Basic customers migrate to Team over time, existing Professional customers stay on current plan/pricing (blog.postman.com/new-capabilities-march-2026, published Jan 31, 2026). This effectively retired the tier structure that older student education licenses were built on. (2) The dedicated student marketing page (postman.com/students) and /learn/education/ are no longer live (soft-404 to app SPA shell / hard 404) - the student program appears sunset or folded into the universal Free plan + Academy/LiftOff; no live offer page exists as of 2026-10-22. (3) No public startup program page exists (postman.com/startups soft-404s). (4) July 21, 2026: Postman Academy badges migrated from Badgr to Credly (existing Badgr badges retained, not revoked). (5) Free plan AI-credit figure is inconsistent on the live pricing page (100 on plan card vs 50/month in the table). Caveat: the session's web-search budget was exhausted, so confirmation of explicit sunset *announcements* (blog posts about the student program ending) was not possible; absence was verified by live fetch of official pages today.

### Replit

- **Starter free plan (current as of 2026-10-22)** (high) — $0/mo, no dollar figure listed ('available at no cost'). Includes: (1) daily-reset Agent credits with a monthly cap (no public numbers — check Settings → Usage); (2) monthly credits for production databases, object storage, and publishing (autoscale/static) that reset each month and do NOT roll over; (3) exactly 1 free published app that auto-expires 30 days after publishing and carries a 'Made with Replit' referral badge (badge removal requires Core); app can be re-published; (4) 'Lite build' only — Full build (autonomous agent), design-to-artifact backend conversion, third-party connectors (Google, Stripe, Replit AI Integrations), Plan Mode, and non-web/mobile artifact types all require Core/Pro. Resource facts: development databases are free on every app with 20 GiB free dev storage (50 GiB on Pro/Enterprise); production PostgreSQL storage capped at 1 GiB on Starter; static hosting is free but outbound transfer bills at $0.05/GB; autoscale is $2/mo base + $0.60/million compute units + $0.40/million requests once credits run out; object storage $0.015/GiB/mo + $0.05/GiB transfer + $0.0004–0.005/thousand ops with a 7-day minimum billing floor; 'Made with Replit' referrals earn credits only after upgrading to a paid plan.
  - *Eligibility:* Any Replit account (email or Google/GitHub OAuth signup); no .edu email, no student status, no region limit stated in docs. Credit card: NOT stated on any current official page — free-plan signup has historically required no card; a card/payment method is only involved when you buy credit packs or subscribe to Core/Pro. Treat 'no card required' as true-but-unconfirmed-in-current-docs.
  - *How to claim:* Create a free account at replit.com — you are on Starter automatically, no card step. Publish up to 1 app; view live credit caps in Settings → Usage; upgrade via replit.com/settings/billing when you hit limits.
  - *Expiry:* Agent credits reset daily; monthly cloud credits reset each month (no rollover); the free published app goes down 30 days after publishing (re-publish to extend; each republish resets the window).
  - *Gotchas:* Legacy numbers circulating for Starter (1,200 agent/dev minutes per month ≈ 40/day, 100 AI completions, 0.5 vCPU / 1 GB RAM / 2 GB disk) no longer appear on any official page I read today — the platform moved to a credits + Free Mode system in 2025–2026, so those figures are likely stale. Egress/autoscale/object-storage can produce real charges if your free app gets traffic (outbound data $0.05/GB has no stated free allowance). Routines and heavy agent work need paid credits. 'No card' is not explicitly documented today.
  - *Source:* https://docs.replit.com/billing/plans/starter-plan
- **Free Mode on the free Starter plan (launched 2026-08-18, OpenAI partnership)** (high) — Free Mode is a no-cost Agent mode using 'intelligent model routing' (model picked by Replit; per the announcement, powered by OpenAI's GPT-5.6 Luna). It is available on Starter, Core, and Pro. Core users get 'up to 30 hours per month of chat' in Free Mode and up to 60 projects in Free Mode; Pro gets even larger allowances; the allowance on Core/Pro 'refreshes every five hours'. On the free Starter plan Free Mode also applies but Replit publishes no number — the docs say only 'Your Free Mode limits depend on your plan' and point to Settings → Usage. Routines cannot run in Free Mode (they need a per-run paid budget). Free Mode replaces everyday work that previously consumed credits: 'everyday tasks will no longer use credits'; the older Economy Mode was renamed Power Mode at the same cost.
  - *Eligibility:* All accounts including the free Starter plan; no card needed for Free Mode itself; no regional or education gating stated.
  - *How to claim:* It is the default Agent experience — just chat/build; when the 5-hour or daily allowance is exhausted Replit warns you before a request shifts to a paid mode (Power/Max). Monitor the counter in Settings → Usage.
  - *Expiry:* Allowance refreshes every 5 hours (Core/Pro) and daily (Starter); Free Mode shipped Aug 18–19, 2026 and is ongoing.
  - *Gotchas:* No model picker in Free Mode (auto routing only); Starter's exact allowance is not public; Free Mode excludes Routines, Plan Mode (Core-only), and third-party AI integrations (paid, billed at provider API rates from your credits).
  - *Source:* https://docs.replit.com/chat/free-mode
- **Replit Startup Program — 'Up to $25K in Credits'** (low) ⚠ low confidence: verify before claiming — A live official page exists at replit.com/startups with the title 'Startup Program: Up to $25K in Credits | Replit'. The page body is client-rendered: two independent fetches on 2026-10-22 returned only the <title> with no body text, so I could NOT confirm eligibility (company age, funding cap, headcount), the credit breakdown, the program's duration, or the application form. Nothing in the docs index (llms.txt) or the blog sitemap references the program. Headline claim from the title alone: up to $25,000 in Replit credits for qualifying startups.
  - *Eligibility:* Unconfirmed. Almost certainly early-stage startup-focused (page is a gated application), but age/funding/employee caps were not readable today. No .edu component visible.
  - *How to claim:* Apply via the form at replit.com/startups (page loads in a normal browser; details are JS-rendered, so a WebFetch/markdown reader sees nothing). Verify current terms in the rendered page before relying on the $25K figure.
  - *Expiry:* Unknown — no duration or sunset date visible on the page.
  - *Gotchas:* Page has no server-rendered content, so any details beyond the title are unverified; credits (not cash) are what's offered, and credit packs purchased on Replit expire 6 months after purchase — check whether program credits share that rule. Trial-vs-free: these are credits, not a free plan.
  - *Source:* https://replit.com/startups
- **Education / student and teacher program — not found (likely sunset)** (medium) — As of 2026-10-22 Replit has no publicly offered education-specific free offer: replit.com/education and replit.com/classroom both return HTTP 404; the docs index (llms.txt) contains zero 'education', 'student', 'teacher', 'classroom', 'startup' or 'trial' paths; and replit.com/pricing has no education or startup discount section. The only education-adjacent asset is learn.replit.com, a free self-serve course platform (e.g., 'AI foundations' 30 min/7 lessons, 'Enterprise foundations' 30 min/6 lessons, two courses 'coming soon') with no .edu gate, no free paid seats, and no teacher/classroom entitlements. Historical context: Repl.it's classrooms product and 'student overview' teacher-dashboard features (closed beta ~2016, docs updated Oct 2023) are no longer represented on current official pages.
  - *Eligibility:* n/a — nothing claimable as a student/teacher offer today; learn.replit.com courses are open to anyone, no .edu email required.
  - *How to claim:* Nothing to claim: sign up at learn.replit.com for free courses if the courses themselves are the goal.
  - *Expiry:* n/a
  - *Gotchas:* Absence-based finding: 404s + missing docs entries + unannotated pricing page, checked today. Replit's old teacher/student classroom program (free paid-tier seats for teachers) appears to have been retired or folded into the credit-based plans; if you still need .edu perks, Replit currently does not publish any.
  - *Source:* https://learn.replit.com/
- **Starter plan (free tier)** (high) — $0, no time limit. Daily Agent credits that reset daily, capped by a monthly cap (exact credit count is NOT published by Replit; Settings > Usage is the source of truth). Monthly cloud credits for production databases, object storage, and publishing (autoscale/static) that reset monthly (no dollar figure published). Lite build only (Full build requires Core). Exactly 1 free published app: the link auto-goes offline 30 days after publish (you can re-publish), and it carries a mandatory 'Made with Replit' badge with your referral link. Includes Design Canvas + Visual Editor and web & mobile app projects.
  - *Eligibility:* Open to anyone; no credit card required; no .edu required; no region limit stated; no funding cap. Not gated on startup backing.
  - *How to claim:* Sign up free at replit.com; Agent usage and remaining allowance show in Settings > Usage; publish one app via Lite build or Design Canvas.
  - *Expiry:* No time limit; daily Agent and monthly cloud allowances reset daily/monthly; the single published app goes offline after 30 days.
  - *Gotchas:* Exact credit amounts are not published (check Settings > Usage). 'Free Mode' is NOT a standalone free plan (see below). As of 2026 the public pricing page (replit.com/pricing) no longer lists a free Starter card - it now opens on Core ($18 billed annually / $20 monthly); a third-party pricing tracker reports the free Starter plan was dropped from the pricing page between Aug 28 and Sept 11, 2026. Docs still list Starter as an active free tier, so it remains available but is no longer the advertised self-serve entry point (a new signup now lands on paid Core). Referral credits from your badge only vest after you upgrade to a paid plan. Connectors (Google/Stripe), Replit AI Integrations, Plan Mode, artifact types beyond web/mobile, badge removal, and 2nd+ published apps all require Core.
  - *Source:* https://docs.replit.com/billing/plans/starter-plan
- **Free Mode (no-cost Agent allowance inside plans)** (high) — Launched Aug 18, 2026. No-cost Agent usage within each plan's allowance. Core: up to 30 hours/month of chat on Free Mode and up to 60 projects on Free Mode; marketing claims up to 30x more creation vs before. On Core/Pro the Free Mode allowance resets every 5 hours and has a weekly limit; Starter keeps its own daily limits. Paid modes: Power (mid-tier, formerly 'Economy') and Max (top-tier, formerly 'Power').
  - *Eligibility:* Subscribers to Starter, Core, or Pro. No extra eligibility; Replit picks the model in Free Mode (no manual model choice). Not available on Enterprise.
  - *How to claim:* Open a new Agent chat - it starts in Free Mode by default; a paid mode only runs after your explicit one-time confirmation per request.
  - *Expiry:* Core/Pro Free Mode allowance resets every 5 hours with a weekly cap; Starter uses daily limits.
  - *Gotchas:* Replit's help page states explicitly that Free Mode 'is not a free plan on itself' - it is an allowance bundled into paid Starter/Core/Pro. A paid escalation (Power/Max) applies to that one request only, then returns to Free Mode. After the Aug 2026 restructure the genuinely free entry point is effectively the Starter plan, and Free Mode is framed around the $20 Core subscription.
  - *Source:* https://replit.com/blog/replit-introduces-free-mode
- **Replit for Startups (up to $25K credits / free Replit Pro)** (medium) — Launched live April 23, 2026. Two tiers: (1) Up to $25,000 in Replit credits for startups backed by a 'Select Partner' fund or accelerator - a partner offer (SVB) lists $25K credits with 6-month validity plus a dedicated onboarding call, priority support community, and co-marketing. (2) Free Replit Pro for startups backed by any other recognized fund/accelerator in the broader 'Partner Network' - a partner offer (JPMorgan) lists 1 year of Pro (free for the duration of the partnership): $100/month credits, access to the most powerful models, private deployments, up to 15 collaborators and 50 viewers, 28-day database restore, premium support. No fixed company-age or funding cap is stated publicly; partner pages say 'eligible at any funding stage' - the gate is being backed by a partner fund/accelerator, not a headcount/dollar threshold.
  - *Eligibility:* VC / fund / accelerator-backed startups (not .edu-based). Backed by a Select Partner fund -> up to $25K credits; backed by any other Partner Network fund/accelerator -> free Pro tier. Requirements: apply with a professional (work) email + company domain; one application per company. Unbacked founders may still apply and are reviewed; Replit may add your fund to the Partner Network.
  - *How to claim:* Apply at replit.com/startups with a professional email + domain, complete the application, and redeem via the partner link. If your fund is not listed, use replit.com/startups/fund-not-listed to request it be added, then apply.
  - *Expiry:* Credits: ~6-month validity on the $25K tier (per partner offer). Free Pro: 'for the duration of the partnership' (1 year on partner offer).
  - *Gotchas:* CC not required to receive the perk, but a Replit account is needed to redeem. The $25K tier is reserved for 'Select Partners' only - most funds land in the free-Pro tier. Exact specs differ by fund partner (SVB vs JPMorgan pages disagree on term length). Program existence + cap + apply-anyway path are confirmed high-confidence on Replit's own pages; the tier breakdown / validity / Pro specs came from third-party partner offer pages, so treat those specifics as medium. Unbacked/solo founders are NOT the target (use Race to Revenue instead).
  - *Source:* https://replit.com/startups
- **Race to Revenue (builder program: $10K credits + $25K cash prize)** (medium) — Pilot launched Dec 9, 2025. 8-week structured program; 20 builders per cohort (pilot cohort ~21 founders across 4 continents). Each selected builder receives $10,000 in Replit Agent + Cloud credits. The winner gets a $25,000 CASH prize at the 2-day Final Showcase capstone in San Francisco. Zero equity. Partner perks: Ramp (corporate cards/expense tools), Stripe Atlas (free incorporation ~$500 value + $2,500 Stripe credits + up to $50K partner perks), Linear 6 months free, Rippling 6 months free, ElevenLabs 12 months free (600+ hrs), HubSpot 90% off, Make Industry toolkit, Gunderson Dettmer legal.
  - *Eligibility:* Solo builders and early-stage/bootstrapped founders who are building on or deeply integrating with Replit. No prior revenue required to apply; no funding/backing requirement. Must commit to full 8-week participation (miss >1 week = dropped) and travel to SF for the showcase; international applicants allowed (travel logistics on them).
  - *How to claim:* Apply at replit.com/racetorevenue: provide name/email/location/team size, what you're building + ICP, a traction signal, whether you build on Replit, 6-week availability + SF travel, and a 2-minute video explaining the project and why you're the one to build it.
  - *Expiry:* Cohort-based, 8 weeks; explicitly a pilot/iterating program. Credits and prize allocated per cohort.
  - *Gotchas:* You are FILMED (it is a documentary series) and you pitch live on camera in SF. The $10K is Replit-platform credits, not cash; only the winner receives the $25K cash prize. Partner perks are limited/subject to partner terms. It is a competitive selection, not an open grant.
  - *Source:* https://replit.com/racetorevenue/faq
- **Replit for Education (students & educators)** (low) ⚠ low confidence: verify before claiming — Replit runs an education program for students and educators; the pricing docs FAQ confirms it and describes it as offering 'discounted access to eligible students,' noting the education page is the source of truth. The live page is replit.com/edu (title 'Replit for Education'). Exact terms - free vs discounted, .edu requirement, credit amounts, and enrollment steps - could NOT be read this run: the page is JS-rendered (WebFetch returned only its title) and the session web-search budget was exhausted.
  - *Eligibility:* Students and educators; a .edu email is likely required but unconfirmed this run. Which roles (K-12, higher-ed, teachers vs students) and exact eligibility are unconfirmed.
  - *How to claim:* Enroll at replit.com/edu (terms are gated behind the live page).
  - *Expiry:* Unknown (page not readable this run).
  - *Gotchas:* Do not assume specific credit amounts - Replit's docs state education eligibility/discounts 'may change' and defer to the education page. Historical 'free Core for students/teachers' terms were not verifiable today. Re-verify by reading replit.com/edu directly before citing numbers.
  - *Source:* https://replit.com/edu
- **No time-limited free trial for Core/Pro (clarification)** (high) — Replit's pricing docs state explicitly that there is no time-limited free trial for Core or Pro; the free Starter plan is the 'no-cost, no time limit' on-ramp. Third-party partner channels do run limited-time Core promos (e.g., a 'Startup Grind x Replit Core' page advertising 3 months of Core free for new accounts only), which are separate, time-boxed, new-account-only partner offers - not a standing free tier.
  - *Eligibility:* Clarification, not a grant. Partner Core promos require a new Replit account; professional (non-personal) email required on some partner forms.
  - *How to claim:* For a no-cost start, use the free Starter plan. Partner Core promos are claimed only via the specific partner link while the promo is live.
  - *Expiry:* N/A for the docs statement. Partner Core promos are time-boxed (e.g., 3 months) and new-account-only; confirm current availability.
  - *Gotchas:* Don't conflate Free Mode with a free plan (it is a paid-plan allowance). Startup Grind-style '3 months Core free' offers are limited partner promos, not a recurring program - verify they are still live before relying on them.
  - *Source:* https://docs.replit.com/help/pricing-and-plans
- **2025–26 change:** Material 2025-2026 changes: (1) The free Starter plan was removed from the public replit.com/pricing page between ~Aug 28 and Sept 11, 2026; the page now opens on Core ($18 billed annually / $20 monthly), so new self-serve entry is paid. The free Starter tier still exists in Replit's docs (docs.replit.com/billing/plans/starter-plan) and remains $0/no-time-limit, but it is no longer the advertised entry point. (2) Agent modes were restructured on Aug 18, 2026 with the launch of Free Mode: paid modes renamed 'Economy'->'Power' and 'Power'->'Max'; free Agent work is now framed as a paid-plan allowance (Core: up to 30 hrs/month chat, 60 projects; resets every 5 hrs + weekly limit). (3) 'Replit for Startups' launched April 23, 2026 (up to $25K credits for Select-Partner-backed startups, free Pro for the broader Partner Network), superseding earlier ad-hoc startup perks. (4) 'Race to Revenue' pilot launched Dec 9, 2025 ($10K Replit credits per builder + $25K cash prize). (5) Plan-structure change per a third-party pricing tracker: Feb 2026 the Pro plan launched, Core dropped to $20, and the old 'Teams' plan was sunset; Aug 2026 Core's credit wallet was trimmed to $20 and Pro annual set to $90 (medium confidence - tracker, not Replit). Net: no generic free trial exists today; the genuinely free options are the Starter plan (unadvertised on the pricing page since late 2026) plus the grants in Replit for Startups and Race to Revenue. Data caveat: this run hit the session web-search budget, so education-page specifics and JS-rendered program bodies could not be fully read; confidence ratings reflect that.
- **Critic correction** (Replit (Starter free tier)): Starter plan is no longer listed on replit.com/pricing (read Oct 22, 2026 via a summarizing fetch). The page shows Core, Pro and Enterprise, with Free Mode (up to 30 hours of chat and 60 projects) under Core. The Starter figures (1,200 dev minutes, 100 AI completions, 0.5 vCPU / 1 GB) could not be confirmed.

### Retool

- **Retool Free plan (cloud)** (high) — Permanently free plan, $0/mo. Unlimited web & mobile apps; up to 5 users; 500 workflow runs/month; 5GB data storage; up to 20 agent-hours/month plus 250 AI credits/month. AI credits are account-level and monthly with no rollover; bonus credits 'may change over time'; agent execution pauses when free agent-hours are exhausted. Page states no credit-card requirement and no time-limited trial; this is a standing tier, not a trial. No region limits stated on the page.
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone; no .edu, no funding-stage, no region limit stated. Credit card NOT required for the Free plan.
  - *How to claim:* Sign up at retool.com; the Free plan is applied automatically with no card entry. Keep the org on the Free plan (up to 5 users) and use 500 runs / 20 agent-hours / 250 AI credits per month at no cost.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry; renews indefinitely at $0 while the org stays on the Free plan.
  - *Gotchas:* Trial-vs-free: none — this is a free tier, not a trial. Agents bill by the hour separately from the AI-credit pool and pause at 20 h/mo. Free plan lacks Team/Business features (public apps, release versions, staging, granular permissions). AI-credit allowances (20 agent-hrs + 250 credits) are a recent 2025-era addition to the Free tier, not a legacy free-tier value.
  - *Source:* https://retool.com/pricing
- **Retool self-hosted Free edition** (high) — All four tiers (Free, Team, Business, Enterprise) support self-hosting. Free self-hosted keeps the 5-user cap and adds 'unlimited database management' on top of the cloud-free limits. Team/Business self-host cap at up to 100 users; Enterprise self-host adds multi-instance deployments and bring-your-own-PostgreSQL. You run and operate your own infrastructure (no cloud hosting; agent-hour/AI-credit entitlements are not documented for self-hosted Free).
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone; no card, no .edu, no region limit stated. Requires your own Postgres/infrastructure.
  - *How to claim:* Install the Retool self-hosted edition (builds from retool.com; Free license, no card); self-hosting is opt-in on any plan tier — no claim form needed.
  - *Expiry:* No stated expiry; the Free tier is standing.
  - *Gotchas:* Data/egress costs are yours: infrastructure, hosting, maintenance. The 5-user cap applies even when self-hosted (matches the cloud Free tier). Feature parity for Agents/AI on self-hosted Free is not stated on the pricing page.
  - *Source:* https://retool.com/pricing
- **Retool for Startups program (100% off year 1, up to $60k value)** (high) — Year 1: 100% off coupon for one year on a monthly Team or Business plan, value up to $60,000. Year 2: 25% off coupon on the paid plan. Year-1 Agents: up to 50 hours / $125 of free usage per month; Year 2: up to 20 hours / $50 per month; usage beyond that is billed out-of-pocket (year 2 with the 25% coupon applied). VCs/accelerators/incubators can add Retool to their deal books via the partner channel.
  - *Eligibility:* All of: bootstrapped, angel-funded, debt-funded, pre-seed, seed, or Series A; total funding raised under $10M; founded within the last 10 years; new Retool customer on a MONTHLY Team or Business plan (no annual plans, no Enterprise). Excluded: customers already on a paid plan for multiple billing cycles, and any org previously enrolled (one-time only, no re-application). No region limit stated.
  - *How to claim:* Steps: (1) create a free Retool org; (2) upgrade to the monthly Team or Business plan via plans & billing — card info required but not billed immediately; (3) submit the Startup Program application (HubSpot form linked in the docs and at retool.com/startups); (4) acceptance email; the 100% discount auto-applies to the first invoice at end of first billing cycle. No reply after a week: email startups@retool.com.
  - *Expiry:* 100% discount lasts exactly 1 year from approval date, then 25% off in year 2, then full price (graduates). No extensions are granted. If nothing changes, Retool automatically continues the current plan and charges the card on file. Downgrade to the Free plan at any time via plans & billing.
  - *Gotchas:* Credit card IS required at signup for the paid plan (though not billed immediately and invoices are $0 under the 100% off). Monthly-only program — annual contracts and Enterprise are ineligible. Overage Agents usage charges out-of-pocket. Card on file is charged at discount expiry if you stay on the plan.
  - *Source:* https://docs.retool.com/support/discount-programs
- **Retool Education program (free Team/Business for students & educators)** (high) — 100% off discount for any MONTHLY Team or Business plan — i.e., free Business-tier access for the duration of eligibility (third-party aggregators such as StudentPerks quote ~$120/yr value; official docs give no dollar cap). No agent-hour/AI-credit add-ons are specified for this program. No stated region limits; an education email is the eligibility signal. Exclusions: admin/back-office/operational staff at educational institutions, former students/educators who merely retained a .edu, edtech companies and education-adjacent service providers.
  - *Eligibility:* Current students or individual educators; education (e.g., .edu) email address required. One program per org (if accepted to another discount program, you cannot apply to this one).
  - *How to claim:* Steps: (1) create a free Retool organization using your education email; (2) submit the Education Program application (Typeform linked from the Discount Programs docs page) with the same education email; (3) on acceptance, upgrade to a monthly Team or Business plan via plans & billing. Applications are typically processed in under a week.
  - *Expiry:* Runs for the duration of student/educator status. When you graduate or lose eligibility, 'you'll be notified by our team and given a timeline for exiting the program' — no automatic downgrade/charge-off terms are spelled out.
  - *Gotchas:* Standard card-on-file billing applies when you subscribe to the plan; if the discount fails to apply (multiple accounts or currency mismatch between account and credits), the card may still be charged — contact support. Monthly Team/Business only (no annual, no Enterprise). Former students with a lingering .edu are NOT eligible.
  - *Source:* https://docs.retool.com/support/discount-programs

### Tally

- **Tally Free plan (unlimited forms & submissions)** (high) — Free tier with no hard numeric cap: unlimited forms, unlimited submissions, conditional logic, calculations, signatures, payment collection (Stripe), file uploads (10 MB per-file limit on free), custom Thank You page, redirect, password protection, 45+ languages, and integrations incl. Google Sheets, Notion, Airtable, Zapier, Make, Slack, Discord, n8n, Linear, Webhooks, API and MCP access. All subject to the fair-use policy. Paid tiers: Pro €20/mo (≈$29/mo, or $24 annual-equivalent; annual = 2 months off / 17% off) for branding removal, custom domains, unlimited team seats, partial submissions, custom CSS, respondent emails, analytics; Business €65/mo (≈$89/mo) adds data-retention controls, email verification, 90-day version history. Billing currency USD or EUR based on IP. No region limits stated; data hosted in the EU (Belgium-based, GDPR).
  - *Eligibility:* Everyone: any user, individual, student or startup. No .edu email, no age/funding criteria, no signup barrier noted for basic use; no credit card required for the free plan (credit card is only used when upgrading to Pro/Business, billed upfront).
  - *How to claim:* Go to tally.so, create an account or start a form directly (no signup required for basic form creation); free features are on by default. Paid upgrade is via sidebar 'Upgrade plan' with a (prepaid) credit card. Fair-use overage: Tally contacts you to build a custom plan with quarterly invoicing; if you decline, they give transition time to migrate.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry, no duration, no renewal — perpetual free tier with fair-use guardrails. Fair-use 'extensive usage' reference thresholds (Tally says they will contact you if consistently exceeded for 3-4 straight months): 50,000 submissions/month; 100 GB/month or 500 GB total file-upload storage; 50,000 email notifications/month. Paid subscriptions auto-renew monthly/yearly; cancel anytime via Settings → Billing, access continues to end of prepaid period; no refunds for unused time.
  - *Gotchas:* Free is 'unlimited' only within fair use — the thresholds above are the de-facto cap and overage triggers a paid custom plan (quarterly invoice), not an immediate paywall. 10 MB/file upload limit on free (unlimited uploads only on Pro). Removing Tally branding, custom domains, team seats/workspaces, partial submissions, GA/Meta Pixel, and 30/90-day version history all require paid plans. Pro/Business subscriptions may not be shared outside the registered organization (suspension risk). Trial-vs-free: Tally explicitly does NOT offer free trials of paid plans — the free plan itself is the always-free base, so there is no trial-conversion trap, but there is also no free trial of Pro. Payment failures on education/nonprofit discounts permanently block reactivation of the discount.
  - *Source:* https://tally.so/pricing
- **Tally for education (student/educator Pro discount)** (high) — 30% off Tally Pro for 1 year, for a student or educator in a structured classroom. Exact prices: monthly $29 → $20; yearly $290 → $203. Eligible settings: high schools, universities, post-graduate institutions, trade schools, colleges, online bootcamps, design and coding academies (virtual or in-person). This is a discount, not a free license — there is no fully free education plan.
  - *Eligibility:* Students or educators in structured classroom programs (as listed above). No .edu email requirement stated on the page; no funding/age criteria. Discounts are for NEW customers only — existing or previously terminated Pro subscribers are excluded.
  - *How to claim:* Open https://tally.so/help/tally-for-education and use the 'Apply here' link on the page (application/approval flow; no public email address or exact form fields documented). Discount applies to Tally Pro purchase only.
  - *Expiry:* Discount lasts 1 year and is explicitly non-renewable: 'Discounts cannot be renewed and are only applicable to new customers.' Discounts cannot be reactivated after failed payments. No sunset date stated.
  - *Gotchas:* Not free: it cuts Pro from $29 to $20/mo, so you still pay. New customers only — no stacking on existing plans. Single-use: the 30% rate ends permanently after 12 months. If the first payment fails, the discount is void and cannot be reactivated. No .edu verification method is documented, so approval likely happens via the application flow.
  - *Source:* https://tally.so/help/tally-for-education
- **No free trials or startup program at Tally (explicit absence)** (high) — Tally's billing help page answers 'Do you offer free trials?' with: "We don't offer free trials but are happy to answer any questions about Tally Pro or Tally Business." Their stated philosophy (help/plans-and-pricing): instead of traditional discounts, Tally focuses on giving ~99% of features free and keeping Pro/Business cheap; the only discount programs on the site are 'Tally for education' and 'Tally for non-profit'. No startup program, trial credits, or startup-age/funding-cap offer was found on any official Tally page.
  - *Eligibility:* n/a — absence of offer. Non-profit (separate, not startup) gets a permanent 30% Pro discount ($29→$20/mo, $290→$203/yr), new customers only, via https://tally.so/help/tally-for-non-profit.
  - *How to claim:* n/a. For any startup-specific deal, Tally's stated path is contacting support (hello@tally.so / contact form).
  - *Expiry:* n/a.
  - *Gotchas:* Startups simply use the same free plan as everyone else; there is no startup-tier lock-in or trial credit to claim. Third-party listings (G2, TrustRadius, formbricks/formbuilder blogs) disagree on Pro/Business prices ($29/$89 'per user' vs $24/$74 flat vs €20/€65) — trust tally.so/pricing (flat team-wide price, currency by IP), not review-site widgets.
  - *Source:* https://tally.so/help/billing-and-payment-info
- **2025–26 change:** No Tally free offer was sunset or materially changed in 2025-2026 per official pages read 2026-10-22: the free tier (unlimited forms/submissions under fair use), the 1-year 30% education discount, and the permanent 30% non-profit discount are all live, and Tally still explicitly offers no free trials. Pricing pages now display EUR (Pro €20, Business €65) with USD shown as $29/$89; annual billing gives 2 months off (17%). Only caveat: third-party review sites publish stale or per-user pricing (e.g., TrustRadius lists Pro/Business as per-user) that contradicts Tally's flat team-wide pricing. Note: WebSearch budget was exhausted mid-research; findings are based on direct WebFetch of official Tally pages (pricing, fair-use-policy, plans-and-pricing, tally-for-education, tally-for-non-profit, billing-and-payment-info) plus search snippets captured earlier, so official-source confidence is high.

### Windsurf

- **Devin Free plan ($0, perpetual)** (high) — Free tier at $0 on devin.ai/pricing: 'Light quota to code with agents', 'Limited model availability', 'Unlimited inline edits', 'Unlimited Tab completions', concurrent sessions capped at 10, Slack/Teams integrations, premium-model throughput marked 'Slow'. Docs (docs.devin.ai/admin/billing/self-serve.md) confirm Free grants 'Limited Devin usage' plus Devin Review and DeepWiki access, with usage allowance that refreshes on a daily and weekly basis; no dollar-value quota is published for the Free tier. Free sign-up: docs say users 'can sign up for free!'; no credit card is mentioned anywhere on the pricing or getting-started pages. No trial period or trial credits are offered — docs explicitly state 'Free trials of paid plans are not currently offered.'
  - *Eligibility:* Open to any individual, no .edu or startup requirements, no credit card required (not stated as required; free download/Signup link). No region limits stated on the pages read.
  - *How to claim:* Register at devin.ai (Download free / Sign up for free). Free plan is $0; upgrade to any paid plan anytime via Settings > Plans. No email domain, funding, or age requirement stated.
  - *Expiry:* Perpetual free tier; no expiry stated. Quota refreshes daily/weekly; purchased on-demand credits never expire and roll over.
  - *Gotchas:* Agent quota is 'Light' with no published session or dollar amounts, so heavy agent use is quickly capped. Premium models are slow on Free; the page recommends mini models. The historical ~$10/mo Windsurf credit allowance is NOT stated anywhere on the current official pages. Overages on paid tiers bill at on-demand API pricing — Free simply has a limited quota. Free trials of paid plans do not exist.
  - *Source:* https://devin.ai/pricing
- **Devin Desktop (formerly Windsurf) Free tier — IDE agent** (high) — The Windsurf IDE was renamed Devin Desktop ('Devin Desktop is the new name for Windsurf' — devin.ai/desktop FAQ). Free tier: $0 with direct Download link, unlimited inline edits and unlimited Tab completions (from the devin.ai/pricing Free row), 'Light' agent quota and 'Limited model availability'. Plans/extensions/settings carry over from old Windsurf accounts automatically via over-the-air update; Windsurf for JetBrains (IntelliJ, PyCharm, WebStorm) still downloadable. Docs (desktop/accounts/usage) say model credits are metered per agent prompt ('default message costing 1 credit') with per-model multipliers; no numeric free-plan credit allowance is published today.
  - *Eligibility:* Open to anyone; no .edu, no startup gate, no credit card stated as required. Not student/startup-specific — same free tier as the Devin plan.
  - *How to claim:* Download Devin Desktop (devin.ai/desktop, Free tier, $0) or keep the Windsurf JetBrains plugin; sign up free; the historical Windsurf account/credits/settings migrate automatically.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry stated for the free tier; daily/weekly refresh quotas apply.
  - *Gotchas:* 'Cascade' (the old Windsurf agent name) no longer appears in any current docs or release notes — agent usage is now metered under Devin Desktop ACU/credit mechanics; the historically quoted ~$10/month free credit allowance is not confirmed on any current official page. Free = no Devin Cloud/API access, no shared/collab features (those start at Pro/Teams).
  - *Source:* https://devin.ai/desktop
- **Devin Startup Program (startups)** (high) — Official program at devin.ai/startup (rolling review, apply via form): $15K in Devin credits loaded to your account on approval, plus a $50K matching grant — 1:1 dollar match on your next $100K of usage ('You spend $50K, you get $100K'), totaling $65K value. Also: unlimited seats (whole team in collaborative sessions), exclusive events (founder meetups, early-access previews), and a Devin swag pack for accepted startups. Social proof on page: NVIDIA, Cloudflare, Modal, Mercor, OpenRouter, Gamma, Exa, Profound.
  - *Eligibility:* Marketed to 'early-stage' startups. The page states NO funding cap, company-age limit, headcount limit, revenue requirement, or geography restriction; review is rolling with no hard deadline. No public terms/ToS link or credit-expiry window is published.
  - *How to claim:* Fill out the application form at devin.ai/startup describing your startup; on approval $15K credits load immediately and the $50K 1:1 match applies to your next $100K of usage.
  - *Expiry:* No credit expiry or usage deadline published; matching grant applies to 'your next $100K of usage'. Program availability not dated.
  - *Gotchas:* All eligibility guardrails are off-page (in the application/review process) — a startup not deemed 'early-stage' could be rejected. The offer is Devin cloud-agent credits, not a free IDE tier for the team; unlimited seats are about collaborative Devin sessions, not free seats in the paid Desktop/Teams product. No region limits stated, but nothing either guarantees service availability in your country.
  - *Source:* https://devin.ai/startup
- **Teams 'Flex seats' (free seats inside a paid team)** (high) — On the Teams plan ($80/mo minimum, up to 200 users, $40/mo per full developer seat), Flex seats are listed as 'Free': they carry no per-seat charge and draw 'entirely from the team's shared pool of on-demand credits'; no separate limit on flex seats, but every member (full or flex) counts toward the 200-user cap. Under the $80 minimum, 0 full seats = $80 of on-demand credits, 1 full seat = $40 credits, 2+ full seats = $0 bundled credits.
  - *Eligibility:* Not student/startup-specific — any organization buying Devin Teams. A free seat only exists inside a paid team plan; there is no standalone free team.
  - *How to claim:* Buy a Devin Teams plan ($80/mo minimum) and add Flex seats at $0/seat, billing their usage from the shared on-demand credit pool.
  - *Expiry:* Included purchased credits never expire and roll over month-to-month; auto-refill can be configured (default monthly budget cap $160, in $120 increments of 1,000 pooled credits per docs.devin.ai desktop usage page).
  - *Gotchas:* Flex seats have no included quota — every agent action bills the shared pool, so a large flex team can burn the credit pool fast; not a genuine zero-cost offer (the $80/mo team minimum is mandatory).
  - *Source:* https://docs.devin.ai/admin/billing/self-serve.md
- **2025–26 change:** EDUCATION: No active student/education license found as of 2026-10-22. Official checks today: devin.ai/education returns 404, cognition.ai/education redirects to cognition.com/education which returns 404, and the full docs.devin.ai index (llms.txt) contains no education/student page; no student or classroom offer appears on devin.ai/pricing. Any earlier Devin/Windsurf/Codeium student pilot therefore appears to have been sunsetting-era or simply not published as a current program — I could not independently confirm its history because the session's web-search budget was exhausted, so treat the historical student-pilot detail as unverified (medium-low confidence). RENAME: 'Windsurf' the IDE was rebranded to 'Devin Desktop' by Cognition; docs.windsurf.com now 307-redirects to docs.devin.ai/desktop, the 'Cascade' agent name is gone from current docs/release notes (2025 and 2026 releases), and the historically quoted ~$10/month free credit allowance is not stated on any current official page. TRIALS: docs explicitly state 'Free trials of paid plans are not currently offered' — so the only free offers are the perpetual $0 Free tier, the Devin Startup Program, and free Flex seats inside a paid Teams plan. Note: official pages do not publish exact Free-tier quota numbers; 'Light quota' is all that is stated.
